# Commit 006490

Commit hash: a979ea2853125b829ee696de5b747a0100240892
Commited at: 2023-06-28T11:16:56-07:00
Previous commit hash: 8161b2d53ab1d05f452965657827a772b0ea4813
Next commit hash: 80d21148f68e5b49bea953f8eee152738c950362
Commit title: Add return types to hhvm tests
Commit body:
Summary:
Many HHVM tests had an empty return type. This was valid PHP and questionably valid HHVM but invalid Hack.

This is making progress toward moving the parameter requirement into Elab or the parser and sharing between the typechecker and HackC.

For any method or function with no return type:
- If async + has yield then add `:AsyncGenerator<mixed, mixed, void>`.
- If async then add `:Awaitable<mixed>`.
- If non-async + has yield then add `:Generator<mixed, mixed, void>`.
- If non-async then add `:mixed`.

There were a small handful of tests which I skipped because they were "problematic" (for example the debugger tests which match against the specific expected source text):
```
const DENY_LIST: &[&str] = &[
```
